Recommendations

  • GRC-07 Third-party identifier flows: Establish Art. 28 processor agreements (Art. 44 for transfers), document a DPIA, and reduce identifiers sent to the strict minimum.

Dynamic measurement: fresh browser context per iteration; IndexedDB/other-store extraction; in-page flow hooks (reads, writes, network sends, responses); Foxhound taint-export capture; transform-aware matching (raw/encode/hash variants) against URL, bodies, all headers and WebSocket frames; PSL + Disconnect entity attribution; best-effort policy and response header collection for GRC checks. Confidence labels and confinement follow the What-Storage paper taxonomy.

8. Limitations

  • Automation fingerprint: sites may behave differently under Playwright-driven browsers.
  • Single vantage point (one host/region) — regional comparisons require tagged repeat runs.
  • HttpOnly cookies are invisible to in-page capture; cookie figures are lower bounds.
  • Foxhound does not taint IndexedDB reads; IDB→network provenance comes from the in-page hook layer.
  • Policy- and header-based checks are best-effort and may be N/A when the target blocks collection.
  • Per-site reports display raw leaked values (clipped) with source/sink provenance — treat them as sensitive personal data; the bulk report contains aggregates only.
